# Omenix F&O handover, 16-17 Oct 2026 (consolidated)

Written by the orchestrator (session_01VG1RqxLFUVdvvPuMRYZtDH) on the owner's order of 16 Oct 2026: every agent,
builder or judge, gets its own section; this one document on `main` gathers them all. Agents' own handover files live on
their own branches as `handover/<short-id>_<role>.md`; when one exists, it is the authority and its section here
summarises it.

## How the sections were made (read this first)

- At about 13:02 UTC on 16 Oct every builder and judge session stopped on the weekly usage limit ("resets Oct 20, 10am
  UTC"), and at about 22:40 UTC an organisation monthly spend limit stopped them again. All 36 agent sessions were sent
  the handover order at 22:35 UTC. **Only the O10 builder (013YJ5gj) wrote its own handover** (verified below).
- Every other section was compiled by the orchestrator's helpers from evidence: git history in both repositories (each
  commit's `Claude-Session` trailer), branch heads and their merge state, the repository docs on `origin/main`
  (`docs/WORK_REGISTER.md`, `reviews/judge/README.md`, `docs/DESK_DECISIONS.md`, `docs/CORE_ENGINE_PLAN.md`), and each
  session's transcript where it could be read (15 older sessions belong to another account; their sections are git
  only). Each section states its source.
- **Final check done by the orchestrator (17 Oct, about 00:00 IST):** all 134 commit SHAs cited in the sections exist on
  GitHub; every branch cited exists on `origin`, except names the sections themselves mark as never pushed or lost
  (listed below). Nothing in these sections was taken on an agent's word without that check.

## 1. Orchestrator


### Orchestrator (session_01VG1RqxLFUVdvvPuMRYZtDH)

Written by the orchestrator itself, 16-17 Oct 2026 (UTC 22:45 on 16 Oct).

#### Identity and role

- Orchestrator of Omenix F&O (`exepex/trading`) and its dashboard (`exepex/omenix-dashboard`). Owner's standing rule:
  "don't do it by yourself. assign it to an agent and let the agent do. you are an orchestrator." The orchestrator
  assigns stories, routes branches to judges, checks lane-2 merges, writes decision rows, asks the owner (AskUserQuestion,
  with evidence labels), and merges only after a judge PASS.
- Since 16 Oct (afternoon) builders merge their own branches after a PASS (DESK_DECISIONS "Orchestrator rulings,
  afternoon"); the orchestrator audits.

#### Hard lines (unchanged; every successor keeps them)

- Never place, modify or cancel real orders; Groww read-only; never write to `contactexepex/agentic-stock-prediction`
  or its MotherDuck `market_brief`.
- Secrets never in files, commits, chat or logs. Run git with `env -u FNO_SLACK_BOT_TOKEN`. `core.hooksPath` =
  `.githooks`; never `--no-verify`.
- F&O output only to Slack #fno-desk (`C0C89E8QCCD`) with `FNO_SLACK_BOT_TOKEN`.
- Never create, change or delete scheduled tasks unless the owner asks. The only one the orchestrator holds is the
  owner-approved check-in at 08:50 IST on 17 Oct (trigger `trig_01GD5B9H2R3EEWK85p9mL28Y`, margins schedule check).
- Number every numbered file with `python tools/claim_number.py <category> <slug> --session <id>` (story O12, on main).

#### 1. The owner's end goal (unchanged)

- "End goal is net profitability, but I want to run it as an experiment first." Success = net profit after STT,
  stamp, GST, exchange charges, brokerage, slippage and 31.2% tax, found from data and proven on paper (E1).
- Three books proved side by side on the same data and timeline: **Book 1** rules only, **Book 2** hybrid (rules
  with an AI inside a narrow envelope), **Book 3** AI team (E2, E3), on two scoreboards (A matched decisions, B whole
  systems).
- A daily answer (trade / no trade with the reason) in one Slack card in #fno-desk about 15:05-15:15 IST (E5); paper
  first on live data with fills at the owner's reaction time 5/30/60 min (E6); Rs 10,000 max loss a trade, defined
  risk only, Rs 20 lakh paper account (E7); no real money without the stricter RM1 gate (E8).
- External data captured every day and never silently lost; every failure alerted and recorded (E9, E10).
- 16 Oct priorities (DESK_DECISIONS rows 238, 244-247): **core engine and platform first; no UI and no UI-facing API
  work**; Book 2 and Book 3 engines first; capture all external data healthily; internal data is regenerable.
- **Definition of done = the go-live gate (row 247):** (1) O11 replay green and judged with **zero** strict xfails in
  `tests/e2e_acceptance/test_expected_missing.py`; then (2) **10 trading days in a row** of full live capture and
  daily runs with **zero silent failures** (a silent failure restarts the count at day 0; an alerted, recorded,
  root-caused failure does not). Books recording "no trade" because nothing is approved is correct, not a failure.
  The trial proves reliability, never an edge [reasoning, stated in the plan].

#### 4. The final target state

All three books (rules, hybrid, AI team) run every trading day from O1's single daily run, from sealed captured data:
A4/A4c/A4e quotes, A16 marks, news, circulars, masters, macro dates, global cues and rates all captured, each with a
committed row in O10's log and an alert on any failure; forecasts v2 and v4 (rule and AI books) written live before
their cutoffs and scored; G13 price distribution and valuation record daily; Books 2/3 decide in sealed same-day AI
sessions with the model served recorded, judged daily by F4's code judge (monthly F4b AI re-check); C2/C4 risk on every
decision; E8 fills at the owner's reaction time; E6 scorecards, S2/S3 scoreboards, S1 frozen; one Slack card a day
with M1's weather and T3's objections; weekly LR1 post-mortem; LM1 alerts on open positions. O11 replay has zero
xfails, then 10 consecutive clean trading days. Real money stays shut behind RM1/RM2. UI/API work resumes only after
that, on the owner's say.

#### 6. Critical path to the go-live gate

From CORE_ENGINE_PLAN (e), updated with what merged since [strong for dependencies, reasoning for order]:

**F1t text PASS (F4 hand-over) → F1t code → F5 code (Book 2 decider, orders, exits; F4 first) → F6/F7 re-judge and
merge → E8 merge → capture health (O10 part 2, A14 early cron, participant-OI fix proven) → owner switches (routine
prompt by Mon 19 Oct, routine to 14:05 after G10 (b), G6 v4 freeze sign-off ~22-23 Oct, `live_from` dates) → G11 2b,
G13, F2, T1c, T3, O11 part 2 → O11 zero xfails, judged → 10-day trial with zero silent failures.**

Parallel tracks that must not slip: external capture stories (A19, A20a/b/c, A21, O9 2b, A14c part 2) because their
history is lost daily; O2 round 1 (git hard limits) before Monday's first live run.

Realistic read [reasoning]: with the weekly limit resetting 20 Oct 10:00 UTC, the earliest the 10-day trial could
start is about the week of 26 Oct; it then ends no earlier than about 9-10 Nov (10 NSE sessions), assuming no silent
failure resets it.

#### 7. CLAUDE.md design rules (summary)

- One dashboard, one version; all screens, mockups and the design system live here; backends only publish, this repo
  only reads. Stock repo `web/` and `design/` frozen until cutover.
- Design-system tokens and components only; NO red, YES green (darker when strong), never colour alone (glyph + word);
  **Paper** on every signal and money figure; buttons say **Buy** and **Sell** only.
- Mixed screens never merge separate experiments: books, accounts and scoreboards each on their own; INR and USD never
  summed; two exceptions (Home group totals; Strategy lab per-family "both books together" rupee total line).
- F&O only in India; in the US every F&O element is absent, not empty.
- Every mockup shows good, bad and empty cases (Example states where real data lacks them).
- Built for beginners: plain words, a picture per number, "what this means" / "how to read it".
- Strategy internals stay private: show what a family decided, never how.
- Engineering: SOLID/DRY/KISS, no file or class over 350 lines (`node tools/check_code_size.mjs`; allowlist only
  shrinks), `cd web && npm ci --ignore-scripts && npm run typecheck && npm test && npm run build` before every push;
  secrets only in Vercel; deploys by hand.
- Lanes: lane 2 only CSS, `design/`, new tests (orchestrator check, judge audit 17:30 UTC); lane 3 judge files and
  logs; everything else lane 1 (judge PASS before main).

- 01-home third version still "awaiting the owner's approval".
- When UI work resumes (owner: after the core engine and platform are finished; the go-live gate is the natural point

2. If the owner resumes UI: ask what he disliked in 37 and 39-46 (one plain question per screen), get a sign-off per

   carry no unique work and propose their deletion; leave `story/fno-mockups` alone until the owner decides.

  loss diagnosis") found strong evidence that the 08:52 fire did run:
  - The recorder started at 08:53:17. 09:15 was 22 minutes ahead, more than MAX_WAIT of 15 minutes, so `run()` ended on
    "no mark now" with no alert.
  - The committer pushed the routine's `claude/lucid-ptolemy-*` branch, which is absent from GitHub, so marks stayed in
    the container.

    run).
